) AS s

    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT p0."Id", p0."BrandId", p0."CreatedAt", p0."CreatedBy", p0."CreatedByIP", p0."CurrentStock", p0."DeletedAt", p0."DeletedBy", p0."IsActive", p0."IsDeleted", p0."Name", p0."SKU", p0."UpdatedAt", p0."UpdatedBy", p0."UpdatedByIP"

    ) AS p1 ON p."ProductId" = p1."Id"
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT p2."Id", p2."Code", p2."CompanyId", p2."CreatedAt", p2."CreatedBy", p2."CreatedByIP", p2."DeletedAt", p2."DeletedBy", p2."Description", p2."IsActive", p2."IsDeleted", p2."Name", p2."TenantId", p2."UpdatedAt", p2."UpdatedBy", p2."UpdatedByIP"

    LIMIT 1
) AS s

    SELECT p4."Id", p4."CapMaterialId", p4."CapUsage", p4."CapWastage", p4."CasesProduced", p4."CompanyId", p4."CreatedAt", p4."CreatedBy", p4."CreatedByIP", p4."Date", p4."DeletedAt", p4."DeletedBy", p4."GlueMaterialId", p4."GlueUsage", p4."InkUsed", p4."InventoryMovementIds", p4."IsDeleted", p4."LabelMaterialId", p4."LabelUsage", p4."LabelWastage", p4."MakeupUsed", p4."OperatorId", p4."OperatorName", p4."PreformMaterialId", p4."PreformUsage", p4."PreformWastage", p4."ProductId", p4."ProductionLineId", p4."ProductionSessionId", p4."Shift", p4."ShrinkMaterialId", p4."ShrinkUsage", p4."ShrinkWastage", p4."TenantId", p4."Time", p4."UpdatedAt", p4."UpdatedBy", p4."UpdatedByIP"
    FROM aquora_tenant_code_company."ProductionEntries" AS p4
    WHERE NOT (p4."IsDeleted")
) AS p5 ON s."Id" = p5."ProductionSessionId"

) AS s

    SELECT p."Id", p."BatchNumber", p."CompanyId", p."CreatedAt", p."CreatedBy", p."CreatedByIP", p."DeletedAt", p."DeletedBy", p."EndedAt", p."IsDeleted", p."OperatorId", p."OperatorName", p."ProductId", p."ProductionLineId", p."Remarks", p."Shift", p."StartedAt", p."Status", p."TenantId", p."TotalCasesProduced", p."UpdatedAt", p."UpdatedBy", p."UpdatedByIP", p1."Id" AS "Id0", p1."BrandId", p1."CreatedAt" AS "CreatedAt0", p1."CreatedBy" AS "CreatedBy0", p1."CreatedByIP" AS "CreatedByIP0", p1."CurrentStock", p1."DeletedAt" AS "DeletedAt0", p1."DeletedBy" AS "DeletedBy0", p1."IsActive", p1."IsDeleted" AS "IsDeleted0", p1."Name", p1."SKU", p1."UpdatedAt" AS "UpdatedAt0", p1."UpdatedBy" AS "UpdatedBy0", p1."UpdatedByIP" AS "UpdatedByIP0", p3."Id" AS "Id1", p3."Code", p3."CompanyId" AS "CompanyId0", p3."CreatedAt" AS "CreatedAt1", p3."CreatedBy" AS "CreatedBy1", p3."CreatedByIP" AS "CreatedByIP1", p3."DeletedAt" AS "DeletedAt1", p3."DeletedBy" AS "DeletedBy1", p3."Description", p3."IsActive" AS "IsActive0", p3."IsDeleted" AS "IsDeleted1", p3."Name" AS "Name0", p3."TenantId" AS "TenantId0", p3."UpdatedAt" AS "UpdatedAt1", p3."UpdatedBy" AS "UpdatedBy1", p3."UpdatedByIP" AS "UpdatedByIP1"
    FROM aquora_tenant_code_company."ProductionSessions" AS p
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT p0."Id", p0."BrandId", p0."CreatedAt", p0."CreatedBy", p0."CreatedByIP", p0."CurrentStock", p0."DeletedAt", p0."DeletedBy", p0."IsActive", p0."IsDeleted", p0."Name", p0."SKU", p0."UpdatedAt", p0."UpdatedBy", p0."UpdatedByIP"
        FROM aquora_tenant_code_company."Products" AS p0
        WHERE NOT (p0."IsDeleted")
    ) AS p1 ON p."ProductId" = p1."Id"
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT p2."Id", p2."Code", p2."CompanyId", p2."CreatedAt", p2."CreatedBy", p2."CreatedByIP", p2."DeletedAt", p2."DeletedBy", p2."Description", p2."IsActive", p2."IsDeleted", p2."Name", p2."TenantId", p2."UpdatedAt", p2."UpdatedBy", p2."UpdatedByIP"
        FROM aquora_tenant_code_company."ProductionLines" AS p2
        WHERE NOT (p2."IsDeleted")
    ) AS p3 ON p."ProductionLineId" = p3."Id"
    WHERE NOT (p."IsDeleted") AND p."ProductionLineId" = @lineId AND p."Status" = 'Running' AND NOT (p."IsDeleted")
    LIMIT 1
) AS s
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT p4."Id", p4."CapMaterialId", p4."CapUsage", p4."CapWastage", p4."CasesProduced", p4."CompanyId", p4."CreatedAt", p4."CreatedBy", p4."CreatedByIP", p4."Date", p4."DeletedAt", p4."DeletedBy", p4."GlueMaterialId", p4."GlueUsage", p4."InkUsed", p4."InventoryMovementIds", p4."IsDeleted", p4."LabelMaterialId", p4."LabelUsage", p4."LabelWastage", p4."MakeupUsed", p4."OperatorId", p4."OperatorName", p4."PreformMaterialId", p4."PreformUsage", p4."PreformWastage", p4."ProductId", p4."ProductionLineId", p4."ProductionSessionId", p4."Shift", p4."ShrinkMaterialId", p4."ShrinkUsage", p4."ShrinkWastage", p4."TenantId", p4."Time", p4."UpdatedAt", p4."UpdatedBy", p4."UpdatedByIP"
    FROM aquora_tenant_code_company."ProductionEntries" AS p4
    WHERE NOT (p4."IsDeleted")
) AS p5 ON s."Id" = p5."ProductionSessionId"
ORDER BY s."Id", s."Id0", s."Id1"
